# Verifier coverage of the endpoints changed since the last release (2026-10-21)

Release gate (Andrew, 2026-10-21): every endpoint that is new or changed since the last published release is reached by a verifier lane and by a fault-injection arm the verifier or a check provably catches. Range `fc8d425fb..354b8049a`. Generated by `tools/verify_coverage_audit.py` (static: the export walk of `tools/public_runtime_inventory.py`, `tools/lane_select.py`'s Mojo definition graph, and a scoped name closure from each lane's own code); the rows marked by hand are the `ANNOTATIONS` table in that file, each checked in the source.

Regenerate:

```sh
python3 tools/verify_coverage_audit.py --base fc8d425fb --json <audit.json> --markdown docs/plans/VERIFY_COVERAGE_20261021.md
```

## Summary

Native entries (one row per registration; a GPU binding and its host twin are two rows):

| status | rows |
|---|---|
| covered | 1049 |

| n/a | 212 |

| total | 1284 |

| arm | rows |
|---|---|
| armed | 995 |
| n/a | 71 |

Public Python methods whose call path changed: 51 covered 48, covered (new lane) 3.

A lane that reaches an entry `covered` is recorded unless it is one of the new lanes below, which read OWED until a release record admits them.

## New lanes (tools/identity_lanes/coverage.py)

Each ran 9 of 9 fixtures STABLE at `--repeats 2` on the Mac (Metal) build of integ/20261021; parts are Mojo FNV-1a64 digests (`verify_digest`). Declared in their family's training lanes with the pending reason `no reference`.

- `cross-vendor-fold`: mojolearn.cross_vendor ordered_fold and fold_pair: `fold_pair_f32`, `cross_vendor_call`
- `gp-lml-theta`: GaussianProcessRegressor.log_marginal_likelihood(theta, eval_gradient): `gp_theta_params`, `gpr_lml_grad`
- `hf-causal-lm-stream`: CausalLM.stream and models.GenerationStream.read, greedy and sampled
- `ivf-short-fill`: IVFIndex.search with fewer candidates than k: the short fill on the device search and the host column (`ivf_out_of_bounds_distance`)
- `safetensors-widen-f16`: float16 checkpoint weights (models.safetensors.widen_f16): `from_f16`, device and host
- `sequence-device-tensor`: resident sequence tensors (`seq_tensor_alloc`, `seq_tensor_upload`, `seq_tensor_download`, `seq_tensor_copy`) through RMSprop
- `sequence-export-roundtrip`: Sequential.export, Sequential.load, predict and predict_proba
- `sequence-moe-backward`: MoEBlock.backward: `moe_backward`
- `sequence-onnx-import`: mojolearn.import_model on an ONNX file: `onnx_import`, the Mojo ONNX reader
- `sequence-program-save-load`: ops.Program.save and Session.load_program (the public `ops.Program` class)
- `transformer-decode-load-state`: TransformerDecodeSession.load_state: `transformer_decode_session_load_state`

## Fault-injection arms added (tools/identity_lanes/coverage_sabotage.tsv)

One source patch per changed kernel file that no arm reached, each a value fault in the changed definitions (a float one ulp off, an index permuted, a byte flipped), applied only to a scratch copy. `python3 tools/coverage_sabotage.py plan --arm <arm>` prints the build and the two identity_break runs, and `check` requires every named lane to read MOVED or REFUSED on the sabotaged build. RUN OWED: no arm has been built or run yet (lanes never compile); `apply-check` passes on this commit.

- `chunked_lm_head_v2_loss`, `chunked_lm_head_v2_train`, `mlp_sessions`, `neural_rng`, `optimizer_finalize`, `samba_head_loss`: migration lane on python/mojolearn/_training_impl.py (running): lane owed after it lands; tools/lane_accounting.py DECLARED_LANELESS already names training.chunked_lm_head_loss and training.mlp_inference_sessions

Sabotage arms still owed:

- `mamba3_prefill_session_export_weights`: migration lane on mamba/ (running): the arm for mamba/host/mamba_host_shell_impl.mojo:_m3_owned_export_run is owed after it lands
- `mamba3_prefill_session_install_weights`: migration lane on mamba/ (running): the arm for mamba/host/mamba_host_shell_impl.mojo:_m3_owned_install_run is owed after it lands

## Method and limits

- A registration is CHANGED when a Mojo definition its export reaches (lane_select's definition graph, by name through imports) differs from the base, moves excluded (the same text at the base under the same name in any file). A shared helper's change therefore marks every export that reaches it.
- A lane REACHES an entry when the entry's name is in the lane's scoped closure: the lane function, its helpers and property probes (the harness's shared helpers excluded), the package definitions those name (resolved through imports, methods of reached classes by attribute name, import-time calls, module tables, `name + "_suffix"` composition), or the public method walk of tools/public_runtime_inventory.py. It is static: a reach is a call path, not a run. The new lanes were run; the rest rest on their recorded cells.
- A row is ARMED when a definition in its export's closure reads a `MOJOLEARN_*SABOTAGE*` define or is edited by a `*/sabotage/*.patch`. `CPU gate HOST_SABOTAGE` is the arm the CPU identity gate builds and requires to move every covered part; family defines and seam patches are their checks' arms; coverage arms are this audit's (RUN OWED). Definitions with no value to fault are listed with the reason in tools/identity_lanes/coverage_sabotage_na.tsv.
- `n/a` lanes: no Python caller in the package, the fast tier (the verifier refuses fast), a verifier or build probe, or a dead helper; each with its reason in the row.
